- **criar-manual-de-marca**: Monta o manual de marca para vídeo que o pipeline de edição consome, com cores, tipografia, logo, régua editorial e travas de compliance. Gera marca/manual.md e o marca.ts com os tokens que os componentes do Remotion leem, além do bloco @font-face quando a fonte instala cada peso como família própria. Use antes de editar o primeiro vídeo de uma marca, quando a skill de edição avisar que não achou manual, ao adicionar uma marca ou sub-marca nova, ou quando pedirem para cadastrar a identidade visual de um canal. - **criar-mascara**: Cria a Máscara 3D de uma imagem de cenário para enviar ao Roll6, que a usa na vista 3D estilo Wolfenstein. O tom de cada ponto é a ALTURA da parede: preto = parede inteira, branco = vazio, cinza = parede mais baixa (balcão, mesa, mureta). Lê a imagem, entende cada elemento do cenário e julga a altura dele, desenha a grade de coordenadas, descreve as formas num JSON com a altura de cada uma, renderiza o PNG em tons de cinza, confere numa prévia colorida pela altura e valida tamanho, tons, salas fechadas e portas estreitas. - **crim-theory-building**: Use when building the theoretical argument of a Criminology (ASC / Wiley) manuscript into a field-level contribution. Criminology rewards an explicit mechanism, scope conditions, and observable implications over a bare crime correlation, whether the work tests, extends, or adjudicates a criminological theory. Structures the argument; it does not run analyses. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Criminology-Skills/skills/crim-theory-building) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **crim-topic-selection**: Use when deciding whether a project fits Criminology (ASC / Wiley) and whether to target a full Article or a Research Note. Criminology is the discipline's interdisciplinary flagship, so the test is a general criminological contribution about the etiology, patterning, or control of crime — not a dataset-specific correlation. Helps frame the question; it does not collect data. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Criminology-Skills/skills/crim-topic-selection) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **crim-workflow**: Use as the entry point for any Criminology (ASC / Wiley) manuscript. Routes to the right crim sub-skill based on where you are in the lifecycle and whether the paper is a full Article or a Research Note. Criminology is theory-forward and interdisciplinary, so the router first checks that the paper makes a criminological contribution, not just reports a crime correlation. It dispatches; it does not draft content. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Criminology-Skills/skills/crim-workflow) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **crim-writing-style**: Use when drafting or polishing a Criminology (ASC / Wiley) manuscript so it reads for an interdisciplinary criminology audience, follows the journal's APA-based style, and uses bias-free, person-first language for justice-involved populations. Tightens prose and format; it does not invent content. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Criminology-Skills/skills/crim-writing-style) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)

- **criminal-appeal-draft**: Draft a Criminal Appeal before any Indian High Court (bench-specific values sourced from `&lt;case-folder&gt;/bench-config.md`) under Section 374(2) CrPC + Section 415(2) BNSS. Most-validated bench at v0.1.0-alpha is an Indian High Court. Produces .docx containing Main Appeal + Index + Synopsis + List of Annexures + Suspension of Sentence application + Condonation of Delay application (if late), rendered in the user's bench's idiom. Auto-fires on "draft criminal appeal" or "draft cri appeal". ([Wolfgangrush/indian-hc-drafting-litigation](https://github.com/Wolfgangrush/indian-hc-drafting-litigation/tree/HEAD/skills/criminal-appeal-draft))
- **criminal-appeal-planner**: Plans a criminal appeal or revision for the defence after conviction or an adverse order — the right of appeal and forum, time limits and condonation, suspension of sentence and bail pending appeal, grounds from the judgment and record, fresh evidence, and the relief sought. Use when a user says "my client has been convicted, can we appeal", "how long do we have to appeal", "can the sentence be suspended", or "what are the grounds of appeal against this judgment". Fires in any jurisdiction once the governing criminal procedure is identified. ([rohasnagpal/legal-ai-skills](https://github.com/rohasnagpal/legal-ai-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/legal-ai-skills/skills/criminal-appeal-planner) · ★212)
- **criminal-appeal-revision-drafter**: Plans and drafts criminal appeals, revisions and victim appeals against acquittal under the Bharatiya Nagarik Suraksha Sanhita, 2023, with grounds tied to the trial record, applications for suspension of sentence and bail pending appeal, and criminal transfer petitions, identifying the correct forum and limitation. Use when a client is convicted or acquitted and wants to appeal, challenge an interlocutory order in revision, get sentence suspended, or transfer a criminal case to another court. Do not use for the hearing on sentence in the trial court; use sentencing-mitigation-analyst. ([AdvRahulAR/dharmabot-legal-skills-v1](https://github.com/AdvRahulAR/dharmabot-legal-skills-v1/tree/HEAD/plugins/dharmabot-legal-skills/skills/criminal-appeal-revision-drafter) · ★1)
- **criminal-appeal-second-instance-drafting**: 生成刑事上诉状、二审辩护意见、公开开庭审理申请书及二审补充意见。触发：刑事上诉、二审辩护、申请二审开庭、一审判决评析后起草。不触发：一审辩护词调用 criminal-defense-speech-trial；再审申请调用 criminal-special-procedure-document-drafting。 ([ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills](https://github.com/ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills/tree/HEAD/workbuddy/experts/criminal-defense-expert/skills/criminal-appeal-second-instance-drafting) · ★103 · 3 copies)
- **criminal-arrest-review**: 生成审查批准逮捕阶段提交检察院的不予批准逮捕法律意见书。触发：不批捕意见、审查批捕辩护、拘留后争取不批准逮捕。不触发：取保申请调用 criminal-bail-application；逮捕后羁押审查调用 criminal-custody-review-application-drafting。 ([ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills](https://github.com/ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills/tree/HEAD/workbuddy/experts/criminal-defense-expert/skills/criminal-arrest-review) · ★103 · 3 copies)
- **criminal-bail-application**: 生成侦查、审查起诉或审判阶段的取保候审申请书，并按阶段选择公安、检察院或法院实体模板。触发：取保候审申请、变更强制措施、取保申请书。不触发：不予批捕意见调用 criminal-arrest-review；逮捕后羁押必要性审查调用 criminal-custody-review-application-drafting。 ([ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills](https://github.com/ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills/tree/HEAD/workbuddy/experts/criminal-defense-expert/skills/criminal-bail-application) · ★103 · 3 copies)

- **criminal-defense-mindset**: name: criminal-defense-strategy display_name: Z律-刑事辩护策略_V7.0 author: Z律同行 version: "7.0.0" created: "2026-06-17" description: "【V7.0最终版】五轮迭代打磨（V3/V2原始比较→V4融合框架→V5规则内嵌→V6罪名全景+量刑文书深化→V7最终审查修复）。Part 6「罪名体系全景图」以双轴分类法（犯罪场域×侵害法益）为理论基底，覆盖15类高发重点刑事案件（近5年趋势+未来5-10年预判+政策信号+证据特征+四维突破口），15类同层级并列。6个最高频罪名配有完整六环节辩护链。Part 4量刑计算精确化（法发〔2021〕21号参数内嵌+完整计算示例）。Part 5 10种文书/庭审模板（7种文书+发问提纲+法官提问预判15题）。新增防御性证据体系、追诉时效审查、极简降 ([CSlawyer1985/legal-skillhub](https://github.com/CSlawyer1985/legal-skillhub/tree/HEAD/skills/criminal-defense-mindset) · ★37)

- **criminal-doctrine-forge**: 分析中国刑法（总论为主，含分论争议罪名）的任何概念、法条、学说、真题、错题或案例时使用。刑法问题的核心特征是学说竞争——同一问题常存在结果无价值与行为无价值、相当因果关系与客观归责、共犯从属性等对立立场，不同立场各自推出不同结论。本 skill 强制输出「学说对照」：每个学说作为独立价值立场分别给出自己的结论，禁止把各学说统合成一个综合答案；随后给出法考采信结论与理由、德国法接口，并标注每条学说出处的书与页。适用于法考判断、德国 LL.M. 刑法准备、学说争议梳理与刑法证据笔记写入。 ([joyWong-0419/legal_ai_fakao](https://github.com/joyWong-0419/legal_ai_fakao/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/criminal-doctrine-forge) · ★1)
